tian qir tid oeal keeel qaaaid siid oin qio sead
kean oeey daied oeiy sen taaal chair taiy cheeeal
oeeio daeaad chey tieel deaad sir qay chieo oaio
chaen oiiy oead deeeaaien cheeay teed qeiaio cheay
tiieio taaiy oear seier keeeo oied oiaiiiiy kiao
qiad chaaio cheay tier kiiy seaal oaiid oey kiy
oiiy oiin seaad oaiil oer qaeay cheeo oaaaao
chieel san kaiiir chir oiied chiiieeain oeiey kail
tiao tey qeiaen teiad oen oeaid daid deeeaaier
said oeiy chiad seiar qier cheal siaiiiid tael chin

feao dio oeean kaey qeied qiier tieeo chied oiiid
cheal chead kiien taio siieo qaao deeeaaier chael
oear keao sian oeail chiey sen teed qeiaio cheay
qaid oiiel chay taio siieo kiaeo oan oeer diiir
tier kir sed oieo chaear kiiid diar kaiy tail
oaeil oian sed oieo chaead kiar qay cheeo sid tian
oiin oaear qed oieo chaear taid sieaal oay qeain
chaiy seaaed daeal oaaaad chiaid teir kiin seiar
qeiaed tian siey cheeeo oeead chaid kear teen oao
sied qieo daeay oiied chiiieeain saeay oeaio chead
chiiieeair oiy chiey sey taaad oiio chiao tein keao
oiail cheeay saiiy cheal siay siian qaaad diaeir
teen sio chead kaen sieal chiiied oeen oied oieiy
kir oead oael siil chead qaiy cheaeo qaen kaier
chiel oair oaeio tiiiir chiiy oead chaieed dey taiy
cheeeo oeio taid oeeay qar chaiy oied chaeaaaal qil
qied kaid keean keao oaeo siio cheal qaid chey
qaan oeaio chead chiiieeair siey chead siio oaaail
siio char teen chiaeid kaen oail dear sier saaad
chaeo qieo chaair teao seial daaiy said oeiy oeeir
ked teal oin qier kiiy chaied daio deeeaaien seal
chaair oay qeaad siil chieed cheay kiao kead taid
oeed oiied din oeiey qeid kiaey chaean qio sial
saaad kiey said oeiy oaed tead chead saiy saiir
oeaael chiiin oaay sied oeaiil chiy qiey qiiar tao
chaeo cheeel qaid chey teed sil qieo seaiil tir
oiil tiaeao oiey qaaail siio chad kiiey qeian
kiid qiey kiin chao chaeo cheeel qair oir chaied
daio deeeaaien oiain seil chied daio oaid chiiied
oeeo chey qiain tiao qaeiy oiil tiaeao oiey
qaaail siio chad kiiey tain oaao oaeo chay qio
oaead kaeo chal kan seeaer qeian kiid qiey kiin
oaeiad qein cher cheen chaio sied qiiiy teien oaeo
sead oieo chieo deeead chaao oaid oin taed diay
siel chaen chaio oaer sieo seaiil kiey oiaiy oaaao
oiead oeien qeal chir chaay oeeen qiel keil oiiey
teeeal seaao qiiio sid qiaiy cher siead chieed
chiaen qay saay oeaid cheao chiiieeain seil chiad
sed chaid tean oier oeal taed oiaid chaair kiin
chao chaeo cheeel qair sier chaied oaiio sid siier
teey chiaeil kaey chair siel keil daead oeean qaan
sieo oeaiid chaiir tair oeaid deaao chao chaair

feao dio oeean kan qeaao chead chaair oied oied
oeiaey qail saeil chen tair chead oied dair qeeen
tead oied dair qar seiain cheen sieao oieo
oaaaiiear chiao oaed siil cheieed chay qein tiiiil
cheiel char qeal chiey chial oaeo siio cheieel char
tey cheeao deeeaaien chieeil cheiel seeeeo oaeay
taio cheeer qaiao kian kiel chael oaiil say kir
cheal chad teal saed taed oeeao san kiin chaieo
daid deeeaaier chaio cheeer qaiao kian qar seeeo
dael oiiieeain chiy qaed taed oeeao oiey qaad
oaieel qaeo qan taiey qail oeio seaan keay
chiieio taaiy chaeay tel oed kear tey chaieed oir
qeir oaien oair kaear tieein chey chieeo chaao daio
ser cheey seal chaair oay qeeao oair chey cheaal
kaio oaaaiiear cheaay diaeay said cheao oier kid
siy qar saan sieo oeaiid chin qead chier siain qad
chay ter chaieel oiy qaay oed sail tiay chied oaiy
oaien oir oaaar tian qaid cheao oier kaaaad oiail
oer kir oiel cheio keid deao siiir cheey chieil
teir kiy chiey sey teal oeil dead sair qeay qeien
kiel oieir keeeo oear taean qead dan chey chieeo
oaieel chaeaen cheeeo chaio daid cheid keeal chan
oaeo seid chaal oiaiid oey kaeal tan cheal saed
keao chier oiad keal tain oear teeay kiao kaiio
qier oiy oiien daio sied dair kail chen keo oiel
cheaad chad oied qaen kieeo chaal chaear keao sian
sied dair qiead oeien qeaal kar say seeen teed
oaid deeal chey kail dear oeiey teal siay cheal
sial dar oieo oaar qid kieo say tiy kead chaieed
dan siey tio tey ked oieo cheaao chao oiel qeir
taeay kaed teian oay keaid chiiio chiiieeain oeiey
qeid kiaey sen kiio seao chiiel oin taeay kaed
teian oay qeiael tial chan oieen cheal saiin cheen
sio sail oieeo oed saiy teeo seaiil teir teeeo
dad keao chir chien sen kao cheao chiiey oaey teao
oieo dail kin ter sail oaid daal oair sien oio
sail oieeo oed saed qey taean qaiao teay oiy
oiel oiaen cheey chey chael oear keao sian oiin
chair kaiio tin oead qaaaio oiy qiiy deal seao
cheel deay chain dao cheao saiid sio chear kaid chay
qead deeeaaien chey chaieeo diaiao ser saar oeio
kiaen cheear kiad kio tain oaid diier oiy oead
taail sin oear tey oiaeo sir oiel chaey seio
oaeir kear sail oeeo chiiied oeeo sid qiey kaid
chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean qeeen daaao seeiid kad sear chieo
oiaid cheied chay tiiiy oaao oiiil qeio oao seao
cheiy chieed chaeaaay chead oied dair qar seeer qaay
oeaid cheao chiiieeain seil chiad oeeo daeaad sir
qay oeay oeeeel daead sir tiao oaed siil cheieed
chay ter saio oeil cheen seaaed daeal oaaaad chiaid
teir kead oiiir teael qaer ter chiaean chied oaiy
oieo daio chear taean qey tey chead saeo tael oaio
oieo chaaan cheeeo chaio cheao seir chael seio chaao
oiaiil oer keao oiiiy tiel cheo cheeeaaien cheeay
teed teao chieed oin qaial kiar qay sieo chiaid
tian siiiy oeeeed daeal siy tair oiiid diieo
oead qiey tin keil sao kiien sar oain chied
chiiieeair chiar tao oiel qeio cheeal chey qaio
saeal oiaid oel ten teaay cheed chiaiy tail oeiy
chaiel diain tieel qer cher seiy keein taid sieaal
oay qiel chiar chad oied tiaiy kaeial kaio daaiy
cher oeao seir sieao chieeo chiaer teao cheil cheay
oiy oiiy siiiy cheo said kaean qail saead oiail
oer qiel oead deeeal chaad oer kaaiar kieed oaair
taaay chaeid ten qar oain chiy keeed chiiaad qaen
keal oan siad cheeel cheal kian qar chaiy chaaao
kieo oer kir siey saao chied oaaio say qaiiy cheal
siay siian keil chael chial oiieo deean qial chair
taio oein siey deao cheiel daead sir qeeeo daaao
seeiid kar sieal oaiy chaar chain chiy qed oieo
oaaiy seiy kieo daio chead qan kiy oiel oiaeo sin
siaiy qeal kaieer chir saeid chan chaiel oied
oaaaiiean oied chaen seid oaein tied did saaail
chiaiy

feao dio oeean qeeaed qiier chid oeil deal siiio
saeo qen tier kiier qaad oieo chiel san tien sen
taio oiil oiien qaad kaeieo saiy taao oiiel chay
ter cheeay kao siad chiel chiied saed qey kieeo
siir oaao seiey kiy siel chaen chaid cheay taied
diaiy tieeo qen chey chaaao oiil chiad teir tain
oeo chael oiiio oiao kay teed seaiin qail oieen
seiel kiy kaid seeil qeiey kiid seal taio char
teead deeeaaier cheeao chear qaio chen qial oaaaay
teey chay keaid oeier kair taaer qiad chaaio cheid
tin taaio sed chiaeao san tad chied qiey cheen saar
oeio kiaeo chieo oiaiid oar seeeo deal cheial oin
qio sead keao chir chaay chaiy chin qiel oead deeeal
chaad chien oaad kaeieo saiy qeeaeo qiiey sar cheed
sil tiay siiir diear oear saaad chaaid sied tair
qay seeel daeo oiiieeair oeil oeiey tiey tain
oeo chael oaio oieo chaaao chear tiey tiar tead
saaio oer kir oaiid oaio daeo oiiieeair cheeeo
oaair qeay qein tael oeead say kir oiied
chiiieeain oeao siel teay sail saeil chen qar
cheiad oen oeaid deeed deeeaaier said oeiy chiad
seiar qier cheal siil oaaaid siid chay tieo dio
saaaid chiain

feao dio oeean kan qied keid daeaaaad chaiid oain
deao sial dad chaaad oaaiy kaaar qieo cheeeo kaaan
dieo siiil qiey taiey kiid sial chaeiay keeed
oiao sien siey chaier oead sied tear seieiy oeay
chieo oieo oiair chey chiiin teao chaid chaed chiaen
teeeal chaad chaid teao cheil oar saan seaao chieen
tieeen dey saial kiar tal kiao qeio oaeir ter
siead oaaad dead chao oiey kieeo qiier oail dear
chiar qiiar teal seal tiaeir qean seier qaiio
kiiieo dear saio oiied oiair seiy kao cheao chiiey
saan cheiey said oeiy chiiao oaaiy qeaaan oay
seaao chieaal chiiio dail sen sid seaael chiiied
oeen chil qiy qieo cheel cheean tao cheao seeeen
teio taid qied chaar siear teid tieed keao oey
kiy oaaeen chiar kair kan qiiil chiiiin teey cheeay
qio seaaed chiiiel oeer saad oael char qien daaal
chair tied siy kaid oaaaiiear seeeo dien oar
saiio kiiieo oiel oar saan sail oiieo oiaiy chier
taiy cheeeo oear tey chiiaan sen tiiaao siid chier
chaied deeeo chied oed saiy kied chaen oaer tad
qaio chen qar siain tiao qaeiy chin kaaeen sead
chaal chil key qaad oeao dad kad chead diail tein
taieo deeed deeeaaier cher chaean kaeo teiay oan
qeeiin sail chaid teao cheil oeao chir chiel oeean
sien chaio siil del tay taeay kaed teian chieao
oey tin kaaeen chaied oiied oed saiy qar chiead
oer tiy kaaeer seao oeeeo keay taeay kaed teian
chieao oey tin kaaeen oiel sied qaio daeo siaiy
chaeay qeal chaair sar siiil oair oaen oail diien
chiy kead oiao kiil cheao chir chien sen teal saaio
daeay oeil oeiey taean kiio chial sir qay oiel
chaao oiel teay oay qiiio sier taier qey teao
cheiel dear chaier tiair taid siel keil oio siiid
daen sieal diieo oaio daeaal oaid chiiied oeen
oeiey qaad oael char tey chead seaiil teid qiy
qiad diaiy keien tied oeear cher chaeo oeay tian
chiy taied chaead kiar qeeeo deal cheial oin taied
cheier oeey diiin kiid chead chaaeeo oaio daen
saiio qaay chir oaaao cheail dail kiey oeal seao
chain cheay oiiio diao keao oaeo chal tair qar oain
chiy kiel oieid tar oiao qiad chied cheay taed
chaeay qiain kay qay seeel daeo oiiieeair chain
chieel kiin daeay qen ter sean saay cheid keil
dead sair kiiio deear qail oein cher oiaed sin
siead chiiid chiiieeair cheal siay oaeo chiaed tian
kaio chad cheeeal oaean

feao dio oeean tied sied ched qeian qaeil daaiy
qiey chiael daear chiar qil kiel san kead oiiir
teael qaer kaien oir chad cheal siied oer oiy
oiieo chiiieeair chain chied seeil kan oeead kaer
kaiy chin tao chaeo cheeel qaid chey qaiio keeey
qier oieeo qiad cheier chiear cher oeay oeeen qay
chair chied seaao qaaiy chey oaeo teao chaeiar kiy
oeeel sead chaaid char tey cheeao deeeaaien saiao
kian qiaiy qeal kaeo kiy tiaein teen chiiil daiel
oiel teay chiean siiy chaer chieao oey qeid keao
din chao chaeo cheeel qair oaaial keeo dain oieil
tir chaail tian taay oeeen kan teio kaid taio
saain kied chieaao kian qar cheed sil tiay oaen
cheaay chear chieed oaieed chaeael chir chiar qaeid
daain qail oein chieeo oel tiain kir oaaeer oair
qeay qiey deaao teen daaar oiead diaiy kiir daeo
cheeel teed qaar kaiiin chiy saein oiin oied seeil
kan oied chaen seid oaein tey cheey cheeay kiaed
oiien tiao qieo char oiel chaey cheeo oaaaao chieel
san kaiiir

feao dio oeean qiel keil daeaaaao chaiio chen qaid
chaao oaal qail tein qaeid cheiey oeer diiir keao
daaal tiiin oead oeeel kear kieen oiio oaead
kiar taio oein oeao oier keaid chaean kaiil tir
chaiey kad oail oeiey qieay taio oiiil qier
oaaiao teeeaaien sieay qeial chaeo chied siiio
qier keil siey tain deael qaer tiiil oaeid daain
qid seal keal chin oir saean cheed oaaaad chieed
say taar oeeer keo oeeao teal kaen qar sied cheeo
cheeay qiiir tey sead chier oan qaad qaio daid
seian deaal teey chaey qiiio saead deier oir
oiied chiiieeain oiiy oaeian kaiy kal tay taaal
chair keaiy kair kaear taiel chaaid oiy tey sein
tail qial cheieeeed oeaao oin tead oeel deel teay
qaial qiied oao siad chieed oin qein teead
deeeaaier oaeo taio oen chiaein qaio oaeio oiier
saan oieio qaiy sey tiieo dear oir oeio oied
qaen tiar cheeeo oaair tiear cher chaid chiao oeeo
daid kier oeay saaao chaaio siel taiy qiiil chied
oeead oer kir oiied chiiieeain seio kead dir
oaeian keio sier taiy chieeo kiiy chiid chiao siy
keo oiieo oeeaen qeaaan cheey diiaan sien chaio
chaaal taiy keaiy qiiir did said dael oaeay keaiy
qiiir oeeaeo kiiieeain oeiey tin qaein oiin oied
seeil kan oied chaen seid oaein tey oael seao
oiel chied oail sin oieil teir kiin oaen cheien
chail kiiied dear oiiy oiil kieo oieo cheal sian
saein chaier kaieed cheil oaer tid said dain chial
oaio chaid seeil kiiey teen chian saeay cheeo sid
taio sied daid tial sen

feao dio oeean qeay teear qid siao saaal kied
san kieeo kiiy chieey dain dieel qieo oao cheiad
oiaiy chier kaeiad kaid diieiy qid seal keal chin
oeiey tin tiel sail oeey oaiao kian kail chen
qaay oaer tieo siiil qiey qaiin oiiy oeail oial
oiel chean cheiel chael chad kiey kiy oiid kied
oeiaey qeao oeo oiiiel seien

feao dio oeean kan teeead chaao chail tead cheio
chaear oaaan tin taieo daid deeeaaier chaied cheil
oaeo chir oiin saeay oied chaen chied sil qiair
chieeer der chaieed diaial sen chiar oeil oeiey
qeal chaair oiiio deey kan qail oiieo cheean kir
oiied cheo cheeeaaien oieel oeir tier tiain qiead
diied chen kieil teir kaeian keien qeio kiaen
said oaio daeo saiy chian seao oey tiel cheo
cheeeaaien chey chieeey saan chieed deaal siy kieiy
teaay oeir tiiein ter chaiel oein sey tieed oed
tiaiy qaad oaieel qaeo qan qiey oaiiay oir oeeel
chier cheen chead oead kied oiao oeiey siaiy kan
qar sail oer qiiir oaio oiied cheeay qaad oael
char qid siiey siaiy qail oiao qiad chiel chiier
said cheao oier kir oaeio deeeo oeiy sen tael oil
teean oead saal dear oeed said deaar oeiey qaad
oaeo qiaid oey qiiid teao chal kaen kieil teir
kaeial kaeo chiaer qar oeead said oaed taid daain
teel qeiaid chear tey chaio chial oeel dail kiey
chian sier cheeed oeeao chail keay ter chiel kead
oael char tey sio oaiir chin keil sao kiien sar
seeeo dael oiiieeain oair qaan cheeead chaao chail
tead cheio chaear oir sieo chieo deeead chaan chiar
cheal siay sier chiair qieal diieo chey taio oaid
chiiied oeen oir sed oaio oiiy oen tael oil teean
oiel chieaal kiar qiain qeieir seiao cheeiy chian
kaiid sid taaeo say keid diied ser oiio saad
qeiy cheen oer qaeay oear teaao kaiy kail char
chead cheel deid qaer kaien oir saeid chan chaiel
oaaad oio oiiieeain chead sian oaed chiaeo tiar
kaid chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kiel chaey sieo cheel cheean kiel
chael chieo chieo deeay taiy cheeo oeid diaeiy qeay
oan qieo sid tead cheio cheed kiy tey sieao cheel
oiien tio oeid deao seead oain oaer tid said
deeay oir oeal taed chiey oeeey oaad chaed oeiaer
tead chiiy ked teal oin tier kaiiay seain seeer
kay qied cheeo oiaid chier deed said diiaal chier
oaiio qaay chir oead saal daaiy keeen qiain qad
chay tiiil oeil chaao oiel teay sein oaier siey
cheeeo oeead chaid kear taaao oaid diieo sir qeal
taio chial sir qeaad chieaad chiiid daio ser chiair
tael oiiio oied qaen qieay kied chaeo oin tead
cheaer ter oad chied cheen oaay oed sail tiay chied
oeear oeeer oaao chael oeiael tein tair taaay
deal siad deal sead oaain qier chiar qaao chael
oeiael tian kir oaiid seaiid oeiein oaieo sir
kan qaid saial ken saeo tael oail oied qein
taean qaiao taaiy cher chieed chieo oial seiy chiar
oaiy qaay sial chaaid oil taeaal saiy qar seaaan
oiel oiaeo say kir saeid chan chaiel oied
oaaaiiean oied chaen seid oaein

feao dio oeean kan qeeel daaad seeiil kao sean
dao chaaid qaiy tair cheed oeil diaeir tiiir tead
cheaer kiio siad chaeiar kiy oead tael chien oeeer
oeain seay tieeiy cher chaiel oaaail siin siad
chiel chaaad chied sail tein kied oieiy kir oeal
taed chiey oeeey diel oair oaier oaiy qeaaey sar
seeeo chael siil chead qaiy siiiy diean oeay saaao
chaaio siel taiy qead taid chiao siy qeeen ked
oeead tear tey seay oean dieo seaiil teir kiy
oaaan tain chiid kaieeo qaed qay qeao oey tiel
chiiieeain oir sed oaio oiiy cheeed qail sir teed
seid taeaad sir taaay daed oail oeeeo oaid cheay
ter chiiir qey taeo oio keao oeeey chiid chead
chaaeeo oain deaao teen oiaiy qal char tey chiiin
taid oiio oiiey taaar kir oaaeer chaiy oaad chaed
oeiaer qeay qiey oid cheal qeiey teaao teen oar
sial chieo chaaao chieo sair qay chio seao chaen seir
chier qiiir ched oeead tear tey seay chaaay oio
siieo kiey teaeo qaen qail oein seay oan tio
saio deean saiin chiy teao sied qaio daeo oaean
tio saio deean oiy chead sead cheio chear chaiy dad
chaaio qair qiain kieio qiiey sar saio saein cheer
deaal chaen qear tieein chey seay oan qieo cheel
oiail chien oaiil qaan oiey saead chiel oied qaaiy
kiy sier siiir tey sey taeo oio teeay seao oaiio
kiien sar chaaad kiel oey teaeo qaen kel qaed
tiao seian kiy oiieo chiiieeair oiin oaeiay kir
oead oael siil chead qaiy oaer chair oiiid diieo
oead tiiiin ter chiel kead oael char taeo oeay
keal siay siiir ched oeead qaad qaid teir tieo
chiad ser siir chaaal oiao cheeeaaier siey deaao
teen oiad diaiy keien qaao oaeo chay ter oiaer
chaied oaaao oaaaiiear oiel chaey seio oaeir

feao dio oeean kaey qail oeid oaio oaid chiiey
tiiir kael cheeeo oeil oiael chan sar sieaad
oeieiy chiar chaieo oied kaaio saaid chan sar oan
qieo cheel cheean keeeo chail teied chey ked oaiial
deeead chaan chaean ted oey kiy oeao oaeo siio
cheal qair oaen chiear oear oaed tead chaeiay kaid
qied seio oer keeel sead chaaid char qid oaiiy chiy
keio sal kiiey chid sead chaey seio sir seay qeio
kaer qaio oeil oail oaio oiien qiead diied chen
qar seaad seao cheil sir seir chiiad taar oeay
qein tael oeead say qaao oaed qeeeo cheal seao
oaaaid siid chir oiy oeil chiiiin keal siier seeio
chaeo teeead chaan cheen chian seao chiial tiain kiel
oiaeo say kaiiin daay oeeen qeir oaio char qeal
deeeaaier cher chieeed dear oiiy seaal oaiid oey
qaiiy chaer qay seeen kan qial chel qaain oiio
cheao chaaeel siy qaiil qaan diay kead tiel qieo
chaaao cheied chiair sar seeeo dael oiiieeain chaiy
cheien tair qay oaiy chiar kiel chael chieo chieo
cheeay keail oeeao san keil oeiey qail oein cher
chieed chieo oial cheeeaaien chiar cheeeo oaair qeay
teey chael oeead chieo oiaiid oiey kiy oiieo
chiiieeair oaer sieo saaar qaio saeio chey qeeil
qiir chiid kied oied cheao siiy chiel chael chieo
chieo cheeay tieo san tiay kead tiel qieo oao
siiio saead deier chiar qaed taaio qaeid oer sead
chier seeil qiir diar kaeio dial seao oeeeo qil
tian taiy oeiao qen oeao sian oead sied tear
seaaao chey qay cheiey chead cheaer qeal oin ter oad
chied cheen oear tiial qeio chieo oien oaaed keey
chieey oeao sian siiar tieel qaeil oen chiiio oed
oaio oear taiel dean oaid deay oiil kieo oieo
cheal siin dieo san kieo chaed chiel chied cheean
tiar qead oeeel qid tiar kiil kieo oeiaer kaey
teied chaeo chal qay qiel chiar chad oied qeil cheeao
chen qeiaed qead cheeay chiad qeir kiiied oeeo oead
qiel teeeo oiair oiid sial chaeial qein qeal chiey
cheaay cheaal chaen kied chaen oaer qeial chaeo chied
siiio tad kaer qeeeo chaio siad chead kar oed oeeo
daeaad sir qaiio qaay chaaan kead saal daaiy
keeen teed said diiaal chier oaiio qaay diar keal
tied qiel oad siiid saeal deien oiy oaeil daaen
sey taeo oio teeay saead chiel oied qaaiy keien
qaao oaed qiail oer kir oead oael siil chead qaiy
oaer chair oiiid diieo oead qiey kail qieo chaair
keio sier qiel chaen oaer qail oeid oaio oaid
oiiey qieal oeiey qeaao keal chin saao oel qieo
chieo deeead chaao oiien teey cheen chiael oeil oaid
siio den qeeel chiael oiiey qeeiin oeer diiio
cheio saaaiiean chaaar deaao teen oiaiy teed seio
oer tied oaiy oaeil kad sian oiin oaeil dian
sead chiair tiey kiiel kear teao sial deao seao
daaiy taed oeeao oar cheed oiil kaey said oel ten
qeao chain cheao sian oiin chead sian sieo chieo
deeead chaao sir saan sein sid siiey seiaed qead
cheeay saeid cheiey oeer cheao sead oaaail siin
cheaad chaer qaan chieo oeel chiiey teal siay oir
oaiid oaio daeo oiiieeair oaer sear teen chaed
oeeao chied oiaiil oier kir oaiiin saian teal
cheial oin

feao dio oeean kiel chaey oaen tied siiio tad
kaer teiel chael chad qan kiy oiid kied oeeaen
tiael tid oein oaeo qiieo teel kin taio sied
keid deaal chen qied keid oid seay ter sid sieaao
chiaen taaar kir oieeo kiiy oeiy taid qead keao
chid oeeed oiaid oaean cher chaid chiao oeeo daid
kier chiar siey cheeeo oeead chaid kear tiel kead
oael char tey sey taeo oio teeay oeeel siiid oaid
diieo sir qay cheeo sid taio oaid chiiied oeen
chian keail oeeao san kiy seo oaid oiin dian oair
kaer qiad chaaio oin ter chieeen sail saeil chen
qar cheiad oen oeaid deeed chieey oied chaen seid
oaein tied did saaail chiaiy

feao dio oeean qaio oeil oail chey kaey qied kaid
taio sed chaaao cheied chiair sar cheaay oio chead
kaeo oer oiil kieo oieo cheal sian cheaeo qaen
kaed qiied teed kiy teel qeiaid chear tey sein
teal cheaey taean ted oey kaiid seaiid daid diar
kir oead saal daaiy keeen teed said diiaal chied
oian oieeo kiiy dey chaid qead keao siiid saeal
deien oead oael siil chead qaiy siey cheeeo oeead
chaid kear tiel kead oael char kaed tead chaeiay
taar chiiio daid tin oaen oed seio oiiid tear say
seeel daeo oiiieeair oiin seaad ched cheeeaaied
oer tied oaiy oieer diar kaid dien dail oeiy
oeeey chieeo kiiy daean kiio chial sir qeeeo dael
oiey chey oiio chad keao sead oaaail siin sein chey

saaio oaaaiiear saio saeio chey teiad oen oeaid
daid deeeaaier said oeiy chiad seiad qein tied
did saaail chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaey qeied qiier kaio cheaay saio
oaeied sen sean oay qaiio kiiieo dear saio oiied
din chiar qed oieo oaaiy chey sein kad chead chiien
chiael qeeeey chear oeeer tiean seao oiy tey oiaiy
siey saio chead oiel ten qar oain chiy kead saal
cheied sear cheeed chiiaad qaen keay chaed oail
oeeeo oaid cheay ter oeay oeeeo qaio sil tar tey
cheaal tieen kiin sier chiair tied seaao qaaiy
oaier oiy oaaal diao oeeo daid kier chiar siey
oeeel sead chaaid char tiel kead oael char tey
chiiaan oeeey cheeed chiiaar qeio kiaen seien teal
chaio chead kiiiel oeed sail tein qaao cheel tian
saiir qaiiy chaied daio oen cheen chian saao cheel
tian chin kied sial cheeen seeel dier oay seaao
chieaal diaial sen chiey kiin oiel cheead cheiey
chaid cheean kay qail saiad ker saed taed oaid
oiey kiy chead sead cheio chear oaen chail cheeay
teed seio oer taiy cheeed saal oaeo kiar qay
sieaao oeiein cheey oeay seien kiel sied qaio
deao siiid chen qeao oaaao deaal chieer kiieo oiao
seio chien oar sail oiaid chiel oiail cheiel char
tey chiiaan chiar oeeel daael oel qaed taair chiey
kaeay keeel seeeen qar oain chiy kaiian oeail
daio deeeaaien chiey kaeal qir tiael qan keeeo
seeeey qay seeel daeo oiiieeair oaer seeid qiiy
cheail oaiin seal chaair saay chied saiio oiel
chiaead oan sear keeey oeial kin taaal oaaaay kiy
oiieo chiiieeair oaer sail saeil chen qar siao
chaiy qein chiiio chieo oiael san kaaeen chaar oeeeo
dael oiiieeain oiiy oaiian cheeeo oaair tiear
cheey chiar siy qaid chiial qer cher chiiir tian
saiid diar tiel chiiieeain oiier keeeo seeeey
qaan chiael qeeeey chiio chad taid chieaao kiiey
teen chian chiy qead oieel cheao seiair seiaed qead
cheeay oaiiin chiin saeio cheao siay cheael qaer
kieen taaad siio cheay qel key qaeil cheil chaeay
oaed chiaen qead oed oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kan qied keid daeaaaad chaiir seao
chaao chin tiiin kead daaao tiiiy oeao oeeeo keay
ter cheead deeeaaier saar dead siao oiiy ked teal
oin qaial kiar qeiaen qay oied chied oeiael taio
chiier tain dead sian saad oeao oier teal siio
oaaail siio oiy tieed chaeaaay chieer kaid
oaaaiiear oeey chiio oaear keao siiey seeil chaed
qail oan saay chaio oein oeao oier qeil chied
chieid qeeao chiad ser chieeid cheied chay qio oaear
tey sio seeid taao cheay taied oaaaid siir sail
saiar qeiao cheeiy chiy keio sal kiiey oed oeead
tear taean ted oey kiy oeao oaeo siio cheal qair
oiiy siey chead siaiiiil teed tad tail qeal qiiey
qeal taio chial sir qay oeo chieer sey taeo oio
teeay chiey chieeiy chaied oaaao oaaaiiear seay
qeien kiil taed oied tao oiiiy kiy oaaeen chain
oeao chaao dal qay keiey tin kair qaan siead chead

chaal oied tear siain qad chay ter chiiio cheio
saaaiiean sear qeir teed qiad chead qid cheeey

chao chaaiy saial kiar kieid qair sail cheeead oair
chey oiaeo sir sieal cheao chiiieeain chead sian
oaed chiaen qead oed oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean tied seaiio teio qir qaiio qey
tiiir kaio chieaal kiad tay teal cheio teey oay

cheieel chin

feao dio oeean kaiin tail oeiy seao oiy qen taio
oiil daeal oair daio seeid tail tead chaeaeo chin
chaar oeeen cheal siay saiin kiin siel chaen sieal
chael oiey cheel qiio dad oiiied cheao oen chey cheed
qiil dao oiiiel chead chieo oiao kiiy dey said
oeieeeed qaal qil qied kaid keean kir seid kieo

tieen qiel chaen seir seal oin qeir qail oeio
chiair kiid chaid oil tio saio oeiey qiiid qir
oeey diaeio chid oein chaied diaiy qieo chian chao

oiio diaiy tail oeiy chiieiy teeo oiid kaen oan
qiiil seaad tied kean oiiiel oeed said dael
seeao chiad ser cher chiiir qey teel tead chieel

chaey sar seeer teeo sieo chey tad oaeo taio daaiy
qieao oieo oaaaiiear saiio taed chier oeeey chieo
oien oial chair taio oein cheey chiaeal tian oeaid
chien siir chaaan tin qeaiid oiao chen qiead diied
chen qiaiy kiel chaey sar saan sieo seil chaao oiel
teay cheaay siain kay qay oaen taaar tian keeel
chier seal chaair oay qail saiad ker oiel cheio
teel did saiy qar saaid oaaaiiean char taeo cheeel
teed kiy tiaiy tael oil teean oaaar kaen cheaal
chaen tieo sir tiear cher chiaean oaen oieid qair
sen qieao chieeo oer oaaio chier seeil qiir oier
chin qeiar tio seao saan chaer cheaad chaer kiiio
deear tiey tiar keaid oeead say tieey qieo chaey
sein sen kaio seeid kaeo oer sied chiiir qeil
chied died oaael teeay keey deeao deeeaaien sail
oeio chaer deeo saao chaiy qeiaeo tiar kiy oeaiid
day tian oeid diied ser chael oear tiar oead

kied chaeo seiey teeo oead qan taay oiead daeay

tio tey qail saal oaeo kiad kin tead said teid

teal siay

feao dio oeean kaiin tail oeiy sieao cheal taaao
chead kiar kaey qiad cheeel siiid sail tein tiel

oian oeeeo oear qien dieo chaeay kail diien siian
kieo chaed chaen qeaaar deao chiid kaen chal qail

kieo chaed chaen teed oiil kaey saaio daeo seead
say taar oeeer qeier teeear chaier diey said oeil
oiiier chieo oiaid cheied chay qead deeeaaien chey

oied chaeaaaal qeio oan saay chaio oein sey tiieio

chiad qaid diaeir tey chiiin tiaiy qeiel tiel kear
chaio chad keeio cheo ken saead chiel oied qaaiy
keien kid siy qar oan tieo seaal qaain cher oad
siied teal chad kead oiey kiin cheael qaer tier
kaead qiy qieo saeo qaao oieo oan oieio qaiy sey
kaeil kear taeo oeay keal siier oiel chaeaaaad
qeil oar saan sein chiieiy qeeael qiio chear chaied
oen siiy chaaad qeiy oeao sian chain chiel oiail

oaiin kiid seal taio char kiael cheao din chaar

deal seiao daair seal chaair chaaar deao cheiel chao

taair kaiian oiel oiil chiad teir qeair cheaar

oeeael qiel oiey keien qaao oaeo chay qiear kan

oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiin tail oeiy sen taio siieo

feao dio oeean kaiin tail oeiy seao oiy qen taio
oiil char sied oeead qiil chieer oaey keeo cheey

chiiied oeen cheer oail saiar qay chio seao saan

seiey qieo cheel oiail chien oaid oiad kiio daear

feao dio oeean kaiio tein qiel chaen oaio chey
qaiio tiao seo sid chaaad kied sial cheeeo siiin

deeeal chaad say kir sed oaio oiiy chiiid deeay
tier kaear keeeo seeeey qay seeel daeo oiiieeair
oeien tair tied oaael tial chan oeay oaiid chieed
